Is there a known limit for it — a period until when this matter will occur? *Am huwa ma yakunu dhalika illa min ba'di an tafattatat al-jibalu wal-hijara* (Or will it not happen until after the mountains and stones have crumbled?). And when the mountains and stones break apart — *wa ya'udu ila al-ma'adin* (and it returns to the minerals/mines) — these heavens will enter into the *ma'dan* (mine/source). That is, for it to ascend, will it have to become a *ma'dan* and ascend? Then *ila al-nabat* (to plant life) — then it will enter into plant life and have to move forward. *Wa hal am jara hatta ta'nusu min al-asri* (And has it been that… until it is freed from captivity?) — the matter will continue until the captivity in which it is held is broken.

The questioner has posed his question; that should be considered the question, and what follows is the answer. *Wa taraqqi al-ruwahat lil-aflak* (and the ascension of spirits to the celestial spheres). And when the bonds of the spheres break, then *hal… ya Maulai, ju'iltu fidak…* (so… O my master, may I be your ransom…) — O my master, may I be your ransom — *ya'qabu dawr al-kashf al-'azim dawr al-sitr* (will the great era of unveiling follow the era of concealment?). That is, will the era of *kashf* (unveiling) come after the era of *sitr* (concealment)? *Mithl hadha al-dawr* (like this era) — as in this era in which we are — *am ya ta'aqub al-adwar abad al-abad* (or will the succession of eras continue forever and ever?) — or will the eras always follow one after the other? *Wa huwa alladhi ya'lamu kha'inata al-a'yun* (And He is the One who knows the treachery of the eyes) — He who knows the signs of the eyes, meaning Allah Ta'ala — *wa ma tukhfi al-sudur* (and what the breasts conceal), and what is hidden in the hearts, *wa ila Allahi tasir al-umur* (and to Allah return all affairs), and to Allah all matters return. *Wa salla Allahu 'ala sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'alih wa sallam ajma'in.*

This — *ya'lamu kha'inata al-a'yun* — is a Qur'anic *ayat*. Yes. It knows the secrets of the hearts. Yes. The end.

*Al-jawab*. With the help of Allah Subhanahu wa mannatih, wa lillah ta'idih, Maulai al-Malik, mahbat ash'at al-mala'ik. *An qawlihi ta'ala…* That is, with the help and grace of Allah Ta'ala, and with His support — Maulai al-Malik, who is the landing place for the rays of the angels, meaning Sayyidna Hibatullah Sahab. *An qawlihi ta'ala* (Regarding His, the Exalted's, saying): *yawnma natwi al-sama'a katayyi al-sijilli* (the Day when We will roll up the heaven like a scroll). Regarding Allah Ta'ala's saying that on that day when We will roll up the heaven just as books are rolled up — *fa dhalika yakunu 'inda intiha'i al-ribatat* (so that will be at the end of the bonds/connections). So when will that happen? When the bonds break.

In other words, at the end of the *kawr* (minor cycle). *Bila shakk wa la rayb wa la irtiyab* (Without doubt, skepticism, or uncertainty) — without any doubt and without any skepticism, it will happen thus. *Yawma tubaddalu al-ardu ghayra al-ardi* (The Day the earth is changed to other than the earth) — one earth will be changed to another earth. *Wa yukhluqu al-tul 'ala al-'ard* (And length will be created upon width) — the length that exists will be changed into width. *Wa yakunu al-shimal junub* (And the north will become south) — the north will become south. *Wa dhalika ma taqtadihi 'inayat 'allam al-ghuyub* (And that is what the providence of the All-Knower of the Unseen necessitates) — as the All-Knower of the Unseen decrees, so it will be. *Yawma tamuru al-ardu mawra* (The Day the earth will sway violently) — when there will be a *mawr* (great cycle). *Wa tasiru al-jibalu sayra* (And the mountains will move away) — the mountains will begin to travel, moving from their places and fleeing. *Kama qala hadrat al-kha'irin kama bada'na awwala khalqin nu'iduhu* (As the presence of the best of people said, "Just as We began the first creation, We will repeat it.") — just as Hadrat al-Kha'irin, meaning Allah Ta'ala, says: just as We began the first creation, in that same way creation will happen again thereafter. *Wa'dan 'alayna* (A promise upon Us) — this is Our promise; We will surely fulfil it. Meaning, after one *kawr* another *kawr* will come. When the creations that come forth from the wombs and the bodies that are formed — they are formed only at the beginning of the *kawr* — so after one *kawr* another *kawr* will surely follow. *Wa qawluka hal ya'qabu dawr al-kashf dawr al-sitr* (And your saying, "Will the era of unveiling follow the era of concealment?") — your question is: after the *dawr al-kashf* will the *dawr al-sitr* come? *Mithl hadha alladhi nahnu fihi* (Like this one in which we are) — just as we are in this *kawr*, in this *sitr*, after which *kashf* will come — will it be like that? *Fa na'am* (So yes) — he says, "Yes, it is like that." *Yata'aqubu dawr al-sitr wal-kashf* (The era of concealment and the era of unveiling will succeed each other) — the *dawr al-sitr* will come, and after it the *dawr al-kashf* will come. *Ka ta'aqub al-layli wa al-nahar* (Like the succession of night and day) — just as night is followed by day. *Bila tahwil wa la tabdil* (Without change or alteration) — without any change or alteration. *Ya jari…* it will continue. *Ila an yatimmu al-mawr al-a'zam* (Until the Great Cycle is completed) — the cycle will continue until the *Mawr al-A'zam* (Greatest Cycle) is completed. That is, inside one *mawr* there are 400,000 *kawr* — 400,000 *kawr* multiplied by 400,000 *kawr*. What an immense duration that is.

And one *mawr* has passed upon the world. Nine *mawr* remain.

And the *hukm* (rule)… It was asked: this is a very long period — nine *mawr* — how will it… So the *da'i* giving the answer said, "Just as one *mawr* passed over you and you did not even perceive it, in the same way nine more *mawr* will pass." And how does it happen? After one *mawr* passes, the *'ashir* (Tenth Intellect) who is there — the *taflu* (offspring) of his *'ashir* — gives place to the ninth *'aql* (intellect). In this way the place of the intellect keeps changing, meaning the *tadbirat* (dispensations/plans) keep changing. But all those ascending forms — the forms of the *mawr* — and the *'ashir* who changes his place: where will they go? To the domain of the *Mumba'ith al-Awwal* (First Emitted Being). Because the domain of the *'Aql al-Awwal* (First Intellect) is the domain of *wahdat* (unity) — meaning there is no partner in it; it is one, it has no companion. And whatever matter occurs — just as forms gather at the *Bab al-Abwab* (Gate of Gates) — in the same way all the gatherings of the ascending forms, which will ascend as intellects, and all the forms that ascend alongside them — because with one form, countless other forms are also climbing — all of them will gather in the domain of the *Mumba'ith al-Awwal*. And in the end only two domains will remain: the domain of the *'Aql al-Awwal* and the domain of the *Mumba'ith al-Awwal*.

So, all will be absorbed into the *Mumba'ith al-Awwal*? They will be absorbed. And no other being will remain after that? No. Meaning, will this happen in the final, tenth *mawr*, or… at the very end. After the ninth *mawr*. Yes. That is, this process is ongoing. But when the final *mawr* occurs, then in that final *mawr* — you cannot… [unclear] —

[Phone ringing]

— it will be cut off. Actually, he is not… What is changing? …what to do? No, he is not getting the audio — that is why he is saying that. He told me to cut and then… No, no, don't cut. No, then… how will they cut it? He is not hearing the audio. I am saying, let us cut him and then rejoin him. Is the audio reaching you all? The audio is reaching? Yes, it is reaching, it is reaching. Yes, it is reaching. Yes.

[Audio feedback noise]

Yes, it is reaching.

[Unclear]

What is the difference between *Jannat al-Ma'wa* and *Sidrat al-Muntaha*? *Sidrat al-Muntaha* is higher than *Jannat al-Ma'wa*. Yes. And in one sense, *Jannat al-Ma'wa* is at the level of the *bab al-zahir* (the manifest gate), and *Sidrat al-Muntaha* is at the level of the *bab al-batin* (the esoteric gate).

This formula you mentioned — 400,000 times 400,000 — so, meaning, 400,000 *dawr* times 400,000 *dawr*… So if 400,000 *kawr* happen, that is one *mawr*? Yes. That is what I… meaning… There is no simple addition — 400,000 *kawr* make one *mawr*. No, the way I read it was: 400,000 *kawr* times 400,000 *kawr* is one *mawr*. Yes. That is 1.6 million *kawr*. Yes, yes. The total is… less than that. 50,000 years is one *dawr*. The *dawr* will also all change. Yes. See, this is a matter of multiplication. One *dawr*… one *kawr* is 400,000 *dawr* times 400,000 *dawr*. It is like that. One *kawr* — it is not that 400,000 *dawr* make one *kawr*. Yes. 400,000 *dawr* times 400,000 *dawr* — this is one *kawr*. Just as it starts with 400,000 *dawr* for one *dawr*, then 400,000 into 400,000 is one *kawr*, then into 400,000 *kawr* is one *mawr*. Yes, yes. It is a very large number. It is an immense duration.

By nature, God did not give humans knowledge of their past lives. He did not give it. I mean, that is a distant matter — we have come from the *dawr al-kashf*, but we who are in the *dawr al-sitr* have no knowledge of the *dawr al-kashf*. So Allah Ta'ala removes everything from our minds — He causes things to be forgotten. And how many things… all the progress that has happened in the world — that progress started from a completely new beginning. The unveiling is done by the A'immah. Yes, yes.


And the *hukm* (rule/judgment) on that day will be for Allah. He will pass judgment and decree. `Wa qad dharaba dhalika Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad qaddasa Allahu ruhahu wa nawwara darihahu` — Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad, may Allah sanctify his soul and illuminate his shrine, gave this example. He said, `qaddasa Allahu ruhahu wa nawwara darihahu`, that `anna dawr al-kashf wa al-sitr yatakarraru fi hadha al-kawr al-a'zam` — that the era of unveiling and concealment repeats in this great cycle — `al-mashhur`, the well-known. That is, the *dawr al-kashf* and the *dawr al-sitr* are repeated in this great *kawr*, the great *kawr* in which we exist, `wahid wa 'ishruna lakh marra` — twenty-one lakh times, that is, 2,100,000 times — along with its fractions. So 2,100,000 times, in one great *kawr*, the *dawr al-sitr* and the *dawr al-kashf* have been repeated.

The word *lakh* was noted to be Arabic. Yes, he has written it in transliteration. So he is saying that what has happened so far amounts to 2,100,000 *kawr* — that many *dawr al-kashf* and *dawr al-sitr* have occurred within a single *kawr*. One *mawr* has already passed, and in the *kawr* of the second *mawr* in which we now sit, 2,100,000 cycles of unveiling and concealment have transpired. That is the meaning.

`Fa atruku al-ghaflata wal-nisyana` — So leave heedlessness and forgetfulness. `Wa Allahu yuwaffiquna wa iyyakum` — And may Allah grant us and you success — `lima yurid al-rahim al-rahman`, for what the Merciful, the Compassionate desires, that which would earn His pleasure and contentment. `Bihaqqi safwat Mudar wa 'Adnan` — By the right of the elite of Mudar and 'Adnan — meaning Rasulullah, and the Imams who are the essences of those ancestral lines, Mudar and 'Adnan, `alayhim al-salam`. `Alayhim al-salawatu wal-salam` — Upon them be blessings and peace — `ma ta'aqaba al-malawan`, as long as the two succeed each other. And `al-malawan` refers to the night and the day, `al-layl wa al-nahar` — they are called `al-malawan`.

This *'ashir* will ascend one level with each *mawr*, so that over ten *mawr* he ascends ten steps and reaches the first rank — the first station. Yet the matter is that he is not proceeding to the second station, into the domain of the *'Aql al-Awwal*. Rather, he is being absorbed into the *Mumba'ith al-Awwal*. All those who ascend enter the domain of the *Mumba'ith al-Awwal*. Just as in our own time, the noble forms of all believers that are ascending — whether of a lower or higher rank — are going to the *Bab al-Abwab*. We spend our whole lives in *nazar-maqam*, taking the name of the Imam of the Time at every moment: "O Imam, help us." That belief is firm in its place. But our noble form, `surat-i sharif` — where is it going? It is going to the *Bab al-Abwab*. Passing through the *bab al-zahir* (the manifest gate), it reaches the *bab al-batin* (the esoteric gate). And this is why it has been said: `la hawla wa la quwwata illa billah al-'ali al-'azim` — there is no power and no strength except in Allah, the Most High, the Most Great. Neither the *bab al-zahir* nor the *bab al-batin* possesses sufficient power to form the *haykal* (spiritual body). That cannot be accomplished by them. The one who forms it is Allah — meaning the Imam of the Time — who alone can form the *haykal*. He will give his *fard* (a high-ranking dignitary) that great and magnificent collection of *suwarat* (forms). He will take it from the *Bab al-Abwab* and deliver it to his *fard*. And the *Bab al-Abwab* will then pass away — as soon as the *haykal* is "dropped," he passes away after three days. Simultaneously, the reward granted to the *Bab al-Abwab* for his services is that he becomes the *qalb* (heart) of the *fard*.

In the same way, consider the service that Maulai al-Ka'inat — Sayyidna 'Ali — performed from ancient times. The *suwarat al-anbiyya'* (forms of the prophets), the *hayakil* (spiritual bodies) from Maulana Hunaid onwards, from Maulana Hunaid to Maulana Hashim, were coming, because the amalgamation of the *thalath suwar* (three forms) had not yet occurred with him. And from Janab Hasan to Abul-Qasim, down to today's Imam — the one who gathered all their *hayakil* is Sayyidna 'Ali ibn Abi Talib, `alayhissalam`. He gathered them for the Qa'im. So the entire collection that accumulated with him, he will entrust to the Qa'im. He became, so to speak, the *Bab al-Abwab* for the Qa'im, and he will become the *qalb* — the heart — of the Qa'im.

This is the writing of the *faqir* — the one who is poor and humble before Allah. 'Ali bin Qutbuddin. And before his *wali* (guardian) and his *da'i* (summoner), `al-awwab` — the repentant — that great and venerable *da'i*: before all of them, this 'Ali bin Qutbuddin presents himself as a *faqir*. He is a great elder of our community. He is buried in Baroda, under the neem tree. `Waffaqahu Allahu ridahu` — May Allah grant him success in earning His pleasure. `Wa kan al-faraagh` — And the completion was — the book was completed `fi Hisn Saran`, in the fortress of Saran, `fi wadi Najran`, in the valley of Najran. In Wadi Najran there is a mountain called Hisn Saran, and there is a fortress there. So there, in the year 1166 of the Hijrah, on `yawm al-khamis` — Thursday — the twenty-ninth of Rajab, he completed and transcribed this work. `Min man waqafa 'alayha` — from one who came upon it: whoever comes across this book, `fa huwa mas'ul min katibihi` — he is asked by its scribe — meaning that 'Ali bin Qutbuddin, who wrote it, asks the reader to forgive him, show mercy to him, and supplicate for his forgiveness in this world and the hereafter. And though he was a very great and magnificent elder, this was his humility. 'Ali bin Qutbuddin also wrote other books. He wrote a book concerning the life of Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibatullah — on his *sirat* (biography) — composed in the style of *haqa'iq* (esoteric realities). This is the first book of the Da'wah in which a *sirat* is written in the manner of *haqa'iq*. A *sirat* ordinarily contains historical events, but he wrote it in the style of *haqa'iq*. It is a magnificent book: `Al-Bahirat al-Adillah`. In Najran, people say of this book that it is not merely a book but *halwa* — just as there is pleasure in eating *halwa*, so too there is pleasure in reading it. Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan, `qaddasa Allahu ruhahu`, once asked: "Ghulam, where in Hind is 'Ali bin Qutbuddin buried? And did he write `Al-Bahirat al-Adillah`?" He was told yes. He said: "What a magnificent person he was."

He was not a *mansub* (a designated rank holder). He lived in the era of the first *mansub* in Baroda, Karamullah Sahab, and was of that same time. What happened was that the Sunnis of the Sadikpuri sect complained to the king — a Hindu king who ruled at that time — that the Bohras and the 'Alawis held incorrect beliefs and were misguided. The king summoned the Bohras. The Bohras responded: "On our behalf, 'Ali bin Qutbuddin will give the answer." The Sulaymanis agreed, and the 'Alawis likewise said: "On our behalf, 'Ali bin Qutbuddin will give the answer." The Sunni scholars who were present asked him several questions, and he answered every one of them from their own books — citing the page number: "In your book, on this page, this is the answer." The king was astonished and acquitted them with great honor. Then 'Ali bin Qutbuddin said: "Now I will ask you three questions — and I know you will not be able to answer them." He asked three questions, and they could not respond. Much effort has been made to locate those three questions and the answers, but without success. He was a magnificent elder of this community.

There is also mention of a long *qasidah* of 400 verses. As for the great *qasidah*s, it was the son of Shaykh Muhammad Sahab Sindhi — who served as *da'i* of one island, Sayyidi Mansub Shaykh Muhammad Sahab Sindhi — whose sons, buried in Surat, in the middle of the road, are great elders who wrote magnificent *qasidah*s.

This manuscript contains no mistakes. If there are errors, they are only in the reading of it. It says `tammat haza` — the work is complete. And 1417 is written here.

Our book is finished. Alhamdulillah. `Bi lillahumma nas'aluka al-Fatiha hadiyyatan wa wasilatan saniyyatan zakiyyatan mardiyya ila ruhi Sayyidna 'Ali bin Hibatullah qaddasa Allahu ruhahu wa nawwara darihahu wa 'afaada 'alayna min barakatihi wa anfasatihi wa ta'idatihi wa taqabbal minna ya Allah al-ifadah wal-istifadah bi ahsan ma yakun bi-jahi Sayyid al-Mursalin wa siyyadi Maulana Amir al-Mu'minin wal-Qur'an al-'azim wa ahl baytihi al-tayyibin al-tahirin salawat Allah wa salamuhu 'alayhim ajma'in, al-Fatiha.`

`Bismillah...`

`Bi niyat Sayyidi Mawlaya Mansur al-kull fi kull laqab al-Zayn bin Sayyidi Mansur al-kull fi kull laqab...` [a long Arabic prayer follows].

Our second book will now begin — yes, a short and concise work which, God willing, we will commence tomorrow. Its name is `Al-Risalat al-Mawsumah bil-Mulaliyah`. This is a book by Sayyidi 'Ali bin Husayn bin Ahmad bin Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd — the great-grandson of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd. His grandsons are buried here in Baroda. Now, this 'Ali bin Husayn bin Ahmad is the son of Husayn bin Ahmad, who is known as Zul-Jaddayn — possessor of two grandfathers. Who were those two grandfathers? Husayn bin Ahmad's maternal grandfather was Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan, and his paternal grandfather was Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd — hence the title Zul-Jaddayn. He is also called Zul-'Ammayn — possessor of two paternal uncles. One uncle was Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad bin Fahd, and the other was Sayyidna Ibrahim Sahab's brother Isma'il. And he is called Zul-Khalayn — possessor of two maternal uncles — those two being Sayyidna Ja'far bin Sulayman and Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman. That is, Sayyidna Sulayman Sahab's daughter Sitt al-Na'm was married to Sayyidi Ahmad, and from them was born Sayyidna Husayn. And it is this Sayyidna Husayn's son 'Ali who authored this book — 'Ali bin Husayn bin Ahmad. Ahmad was the brother of Sayyidna Ibrahim. His grandsons — whose names include 'Ali bin Husayn bin 'Ali — are buried in Baroda. He has also been called 'Ali bin Husayn Zul-Jaddayn, and it is sometimes mistakenly thought that he is the direct son of Zul-Jaddayn, but in fact he is the great-grandson of Zul-Jaddayn who is buried in Baroda. He belongs to the lineage of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd — the same lineage. The full chain is: 'Ali bin Husayn bin 'Ali bin Husayn bin Ahmad bin Muhammad bin Fahd. He was a great scholar — the one buried in Baroda was a very great scholar indeed. He passed away at the age of fifty-five.

God willing, we will begin reading this book from tomorrow. The PDF will be sent today so that you may study it in advance, and those who can take a printout are encouraged to do so.

And there are two systems in use. One is running on Google Drive, and those listeners receive the lesson and can listen to it at a later time. The rest are on Zoom, listening in real time. Allah hafiz.
